\begin{split}|\mathcal{J}_{2,2}|=\left|-\int_{0}^{t}\!\!\!\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{s}\hat{c}\,\alpha\,\varphi\,dx_{2}ds\right|&\leq C_{3}\left\{\|{\mathbf{V}}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}^{2}+\|\partial_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{n}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}^{2}+\|\varphi\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{t})}\right\}\\
&=C_{3}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}(I(s)+I_{1,n}(s))ds+\int_{0}^{t}\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}ds\right\}\,.\end{split}

(B.62)

Concerning
\mathcal{J}_{2,3}
, we substitute
\partial_{t}\varphi
with the right-hand side of (
B.55
) to rewrite it as

\mathcal{J}_{2,3}=-\int_{0}^{t}\!\!\!\int_{\mathbb{R}}\hat{c}\,\alpha\,(\dot{u}^{+}_{N}+\hat{D}_{1}\dot{H}^{+}_{N}+\hat{D}_{2}\dot{H}^{-}_{N}+\hat{D}_{3}\varphi)\,dx_{2}ds\cong\int_{0}^{t}\!\!\!\int_{\mathbb{R}}\hat{c}\,\alpha\,\beta\,dx_{2}\,ds+\int_{0}^{t}\!\!\!\int_{\mathbb{R}}\hat{c}\,\alpha\,\varphi\,dx_{2}\,ds

where the second type of boundary integral above is exactly the same as
\mathcal{J}_{2,2}
and the first one is trivially estimated by using once again the trace type inequality, hence we get the same estimate (
B.62
)

\begin{split}|\mathcal{J}_{2,3}|\leq C_{3}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}(I(s)+I_{1,n}(s))ds+\int_{0}^{t}\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}ds\right\}\,.\end{split}

(B.63)

Concerning, instead, the first spatial integral
\mathcal{J}_{2,1}
, the use of Cauchy–Schwarz and weighted Young’s inequalities and the trace type inequality (
B.19
) gives

\begin{split}|\mathcal{J}_{2,1}|&\leq\varepsilon\|\alpha(t)|_{x_{1}=0}\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}+\frac{C_{2}}{\varepsilon}\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\\
&\leq\varepsilon\left\{\|\alpha(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+})}+\|\partial_{1}\alpha(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+})}\right\}+\frac{C_{2}}{\varepsilon}\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\\
&\leq\varepsilon\left\{I(t)+I_{1,n}(t)\right\}+\frac{C_{2}}{\varepsilon}\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\,.\end{split}

(B.64)

Gathering (
B.64
), (
B.62
) and (
B.63
) we get

\begin{split}|\mathcal{J}_{2}|\leq&\varepsilon\left\{I(t)+I_{1,n}(t)\right\}+\frac{C_{2}}{\varepsilon}\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\\
&+C_{3}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}(I(s)+I_{1,n}(s))ds+\int_{0}^{t}\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}ds\right\}\,.\end{split}

(B.65)

Summing the estimate of
\mathcal{J}_{1}
and
\mathcal{J}_{2}
above, we end up with the following estimate of the boundary integral of the quadratic form (
B.52
)

\begin{split}&\Big|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}(\mathcal{B}_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{s},{\mathbf{V}}_{s})|_{x_{1}=0}dx_{2}\,ds\Big|\cong|\mathcal{J}_{1}+\mathcal{J}_{2}|\\
&\quad\leq\varepsilon\left\{I(t)+I_{1,n}(t)\right\}+\frac{C_{2}}{\varepsilon}\left\{\|{\mathbf{F}}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}^{2}+\|\varphi(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}\right\}\\
&\qquad+\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\left\{\int_{0}^{t}(I_{1,\ast}+I_{1,n})(s)ds+\int_{0}^{t}\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}ds\right\}\,.\end{split}

(B.66)

Using (
B.50
), (
B.51
) and (
B.66
) together with the counterpart of (
B.16
) with
{\mathbf{V}}_{t}
instead of
{\mathbf{V}}
, from (
B.49
) we obtain

\begin{split}I_{0}(t)\leq&\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\left\{\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}+\|\partial_{s}{\mathbf{F}}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(I_{1,\ast}+I_{1,n})(s)ds+\int_{0}^{t}\|\varphi(s)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}ds\right\}\\
&+\varepsilon\left\{I(t)+I_{1,n}(t)\right\}+\frac{C_{2}}{\varepsilon}\|\varphi(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}\,.\end{split}

(B.67)

B.6.
Estimate of the front
\varphi

To estimate the
L^{2}-
norm of the front we multiply by
\varphi
the first boundary condition in (
B.1
) (we choose the
+
side) and integrate by parts over
\Gamma_{t}
to get

\int_{\mathbb{R}}|\varphi(t)|^{2}dx_{2}-\int_{0}^{t}\!\!\!\int_{\mathbb{R}}(\partial_{2}\hat{u}^{+}_{2})\varphi^{2}\,dx_{2}\,ds-2\int_{0}^{t}\!\!\!\int_{\mathbb{R}}\dot{u}^{+}_{N}\varphi\,dx_{2}\,ds-2\int_{0}^{t}\!\!\!\int_{\mathbb{R}}(\partial_{1}\hat{u}^{+}_{N})\varphi^{2}\,dx_{2}\,ds=0\,,

(B.68)

from which, by Cauchy–Schwarz and Young’s inequalities, and the trace type inequality (
B.18
)

\begin{split}\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}&\leq C_{1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\,ds+\|\dot{u}^{+}_{N}|_{x_{1}=0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{t})}\\
&\leq C_{1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\,ds+\|\partial_{1}{\mathbf{V}}_{n}^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}+\|{\mathbf{V}}^{+}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}\\
&\leq C_{1}\int_{0}^{t}\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\,ds+\int_{0}^{t}(I_{1,n}+I)(s)ds\,.\end{split}

(B.69)

Using the expressions of
\partial_{2}\varphi
and
\partial_{t}\varphi
in (
B.42
) and (
B.55
) we can recover an estimate of the
L^{2}-
norms of those derivatives from the estimate (
B.69
) and (
B.18
). Precisely, integrating over
\mathbb{R}
(
B.42
) and (
B.55
) and using (
B.18
) we get

\begin{split}\|\partial_{2}\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}&\leq C_{2}\{\|{\mathbf{V}}_{n}(t)|_{x_{1}=0}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}+\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\}\\
&\leq C_{2}\{I(t)+I_{1,n}(t)+\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\}\,,\end{split}

(B.70)

see (
B.4
), (
B.6
), and analogously

\begin{split}\|\partial_{t}\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\leq C_{2}\{I(t)+I_{1,n}(t)+\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\}\,.\end{split}

(B.71)

B.7.
H^{1}_{\ast}-
estimate

We add estimates (
B.20
), (
B.33
), (
B.47
), (
B.67
) and (
B.69
) to get

\begin{split}I_{1,\ast}(t)+\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}&=I(t)+I_{0}(t)+I_{\sigma}(t)+I_{2}(t)+\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\\
&\leq\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}(I_{1,\ast}(s)+I_{1,n}(s)+\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})})ds\\
&\quad+\varepsilon\left\{I(t)+I_{1,n}(t)\right\}+\frac{C_{2}}{\varepsilon}\|\varphi(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}\\
&\quad+\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\left\{\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}+\|\sigma\partial_{1}{\mathbf{F}}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}^{2}+\|\partial_{2}{\mathbf{F}}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}^{2}+\|\partial_{s}{\mathbf{F}}\|_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}^{2}\right\}\\
&\leq\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}(I_{1,\ast}(s)+I_{1,n}(s)+\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})})ds\\
&\quad+\varepsilon\left\{I(t)+I_{1,n}(t)\right\}+\frac{C_{2}}{\varepsilon}\|\varphi(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}+\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{\ast}(\Omega_{t})}\,.\end{split}

Then using (
B.69
) to estimate
\|\varphi(t)\|_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}^{2}
in the right-hand side above, the previous inequality reduces to

\begin{split}I_{1,\ast}(t)+\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\leq&\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}(I_{1,\ast}(s)+I_{1,n}(s)+\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})})ds\\
&+\varepsilon\left\{I(t)+I_{1,n}(t)\right\}+\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{\ast}(\Omega_{t})}\,.\end{split}

Then we use (
B.26
) to get a control of the normal derivatives of
{\mathbf{V}}^{\pm}_{n}
involved in the term
I_{1,n}
in the right-hand side above to get

\begin{split}I_{1,\ast}(t)+\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\leq&\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}(I_{1,\ast}(s)+\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})})ds\\
&+C_{1}\varepsilon\left\{I_{1,\ast}(t)+\|{\mathbf{F}}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+})}\right\}+\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{\ast}(\Omega_{t})}\,.\end{split}

In order to estimate the spatial
L^{2}-
norm of the source
{\mathbf{F}}(t)
in the right-hand side above we use the following argument

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{F}}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+})}&=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}|{\mathbf{F}}(t)|^{2}d{\bf x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{s}(|{\mathbf{F}}(s)|^{2})ds\,d{\bf x}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\int_{0}^{t}2{\mathbf{F}}(s)\partial_{s}{\mathbf{F}}(s)\,ds\,d{\bf x}\\
&\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathbf{F}}(s)|^{2}\,ds\,d{\bf x}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+}}\int_{0}^{t}|\partial_{s}{\mathbf{F}}(s)|^{2}\,ds\,d{\bf x}=\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}+\|\partial_{s}{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Omega_{t})}\,,\end{split}

so that

\begin{split}I_{1,\ast}(t)+\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\leq&\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\int_{0}^{t}(I_{1,\ast}(s)+\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})})ds+C_{1}\varepsilon I_{1,\ast}(t)+\frac{C_{3}}{\varepsilon}\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{\ast}(\Omega_{t})}\,,\end{split}

hence taking
\varepsilon>0
small enough in order to absorb
C_{1}\varepsilon I_{1,\ast}(t)
in the left-hand side above, we end up with

\begin{split}I_{1,\ast}(t)+\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\leq&C_{3}\int_{0}^{t}(I_{1,\ast}(s)+\|\varphi(s)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})})ds+C_{3}\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{\ast}(\Omega_{t})}\,.\end{split}

Applying Grönwall’s lemma, we obtain

I_{1,\ast}(t)+\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}\leq C_{3}\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{\ast}(\Omega_{t})}e^{C_{3}t}

and integrating on
t
over
(0,T)

\|{\mathbf{V}}\|^{2}_{1,\ast,T}+\|\varphi\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{T})}\leq C\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{\ast}(\Omega_{T})}\,,

(B.72)

where
C
is a positive constant depending only on
T
and
K
from (
4.6
).

From the use of estimates (
B.70
), (
B.71
) we can manage to include the
L^{2}(\Gamma_{T})-
norms of
\nabla_{t,x_{2}}\varphi
in the left-hand side of (
B.72
), so as to get a control of the
H^{1}(\Gamma_{T})-
norm of the front
\varphi
.

From integration of (
B.70
), (
B.71
) with respect to
t
over
(0,T)
and the use of (
B.26
) we get

\begin{split}\|\nabla_{t,x_{2}}\varphi\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{T})}&\leq C_{2}\left\{\int_{0}^{T}(I(t)+I_{1,n}(t))dt+\int_{0}^{T}\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}dt\right\}\\
&\leq C_{2}\left\{\int_{0}^{T}I_{1,\ast}(t)\,dt+\int_{0}^{T}\|{\mathbf{F}}(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{2}_{+})}dt+\int_{0}^{T}\|\varphi(t)\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\mathbb{R})}dt\right\}\\
&\leq C_{2}\left\{\|{\mathbf{V}}\|^{2}_{1,\ast,T}+\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Omega_{T})}+\|\varphi\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{T})}\right\}\,,\end{split}

where, in the last inequality, it is used that

\int_{0}^{T}I_{1,\ast}(t)\,dt\cong\|{\mathbf{V}}\|^{2}_{1,\ast,T}\,.

Using (
B.72
) to estimate the right-hand side above, we end up with

\begin{split}\|\nabla_{t,x_{2}}\varphi\|^{2}_{L^{2}(\Gamma_{T})}\leq C\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{\ast}(\Omega_{T})}\,.\end{split}

(B.73)

Then adding the latter to (
B.72
) we get

\|{\mathbf{V}}\|^{2}_{1,\ast,T}+\|\varphi\|^{2}_{H^{1}(\Gamma_{T})}\leq C\|{\mathbf{F}}\|^{2}_{H^{1}_{\ast}(\Omega_{T})}\,,

(B.74)

which is just the estimate (
5.33
), in view of (
5.1
) (recalling that to shortcut notation we have set
\dot{\mathbf{U}}=\dot{\mathbf{U}}^{\natural}
and
\dot{\mathbf{U}}=J{\mathbf{V}}
, see (
5.1
)).
